# Admin Web – Scenarios & Use Cases (REVIEW COPY)

Review copy of `Admin_Web_Scenarios_and_Use_Cases` with two corrections: steps whose outcome was not observed during exploration end with "(to be confirmed)", and duplicate titles name their screen. Scenario IDs are unchanged.
Generated 2026-10-30 from read-only exploration of the DEV Admin Web as Superadmin. No scenario has been executed.

| Module | Scenarios |
|---|---|
| Authentication | 21 |
| Global Navigation & Header | 14 |
| Dashboard | 29 |
| Role & Permission | 24 |
| Employee Management | 78 |
| Fleet & Vehicle Management | 109 |
| Booking & Operations | 46 |
| Inquiry Management | 13 |
| Lead Management | 32 |
| Client Management | 196 |
| Category Management | 90 |
| **Total** | **652** |

## Authentication

| Scenario ID | Module | Use Case Title | Scenario Steps |
|---|---|---|---|
| ADMIN-AUTH-001 | Authentication | Redirect to Login when opening a protected URL without a session | 1. Open a private/incognito browser window.<br>2. Navigate to https://dari-dev.siddagangatravels.com/dashboard/client-management/contracted-clients.<br>3. Check that the browser ends on `/login` (page title 'Login \| Siddaganga').<br>4. Check that the 'Welcome Back' / 'Login to your Account' form is displayed. |
| ADMIN-AUTH-002 | Authentication | Verify Login page layout and controls | 1. Open https://dari-dev.siddagangatravels.com/login in a private window.<br>2. Check that branding images (coach, Siddaganga logo) are shown.<br>3. Check that heading 'Welcome Back' and text 'Login to your Account'.<br>4. Check that field 'Username / Email Address' with placeholder 'Enter your username or email address'.<br>5. Check that field 'Password' with placeholder 'Enter your password' and an eye icon button.<br>6. Check that link 'Forgot Password?', button 'Login', link 'Log in with OTP'. |
| ADMIN-AUTH-003 | Authentication | Log in with valid username/email and password | 1. Open /login in a private window.<br>2. Enter the QA account username/email.<br>3. Enter the QA account password.<br>4. Click 'Login'.<br>5. Check that the user is navigated to `/dashboard` (observed landing page).<br>6. Check that header shows 'Dashboard' and the avatar labelled with the user's role (e.g. 'Superadmin').<br>7. Check that sidebar sections OVERVIEW, PEOPLE & ACCESS, FLEET & TRIPS, PARTNERS, OPERATIONS are visible. |
| ADMIN-AUTH-004 | Authentication | Return to the originally requested page after login | 1. In a private window open /dashboard/client-management/contracted-clients (redirects to /login).<br>2. Log in with valid credentials. |
| ADMIN-AUTH-005 | Authentication | Login with a wrong password | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Enter a valid username/email.<br>3. Enter an incorrect password.<br>4. Click 'Login'.<br>5. Check that the user stays on /login. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that an error message is displayed. (to be confirmed)<br>7. Check that the password field is not echoed in the message. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-006 | Authentication | Login with an unregistered username/email | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Enter 'qa.unknown.user@example.com'.<br>3. Enter any password.<br>4. Click 'Login'.<br>5. Check that login fails with a generic error; the message does not state whether the user exists. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-007 | Authentication | Login with empty fields | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Leave both fields empty and click 'Login'.<br>3. Fill only the username and click 'Login'.<br>4. Clear username, fill only the password and click 'Login'.<br>5. Check that each attempt is blocked; a required-field message appears for the empty field(s). (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that no request results in navigation to /dashboard. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-008 | Authentication | Username field accepts plain usernames despite email input type | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Enter a username without '@' (e.g. the QA username).<br>3. Enter the password and click 'Login'.<br>4. Check that a plain username is accepted (no browser 'include an @' tooltip blocks submission). Confirm actual behaviour. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-009 | Authentication | Toggle password visibility | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Type any text in 'Password'.<br>3. Click the eye icon.<br>4. Click it again.<br>5. Check that first click shows the text in clear; second click masks it again. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that the typed value is unchanged. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-010 | Authentication | Leading/trailing spaces and case in username | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Enter the QA username/email with a leading and trailing space, then in UPPER CASE.<br>3. Enter the correct password and click 'Login'.<br>4. Check that spaces are trimmed and email matching is case-insensitive, or a clear error is shown. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-011 | Authentication | Repeated failed logins | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Attempt login with a wrong password 5–10 times for a dedicated test account.<br>3. Check that after N failures the account is locked or throttled, or a CAPTCHA appears. (to be confirmed)<br>4. Check that a locked account cannot log in even with the correct password until unlocked. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-012 | Authentication | Session persists across browser refresh and new tab | 1. Log in.<br>2. Refresh the browser.<br>3. Open /dashboard/inquiry-management/total-inquiries in a new tab of the same browser.<br>4. Check that the user remains logged in on refresh and in the new tab (observed: session reused across tabs during exploration). |
| ADMIN-AUTH-013 | Authentication | Session expiry redirects to Login | 1. Log in.<br>2. Leave the app idle beyond the session timeout (or clear site cookies/local storage).<br>3. Click any sidebar link.<br>4. Check that the user is redirected to /login; no data is shown and no unhandled error appears. (to be confirmed)<br>5. Check that session timeout duration. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-014 | Authentication | Open Forgot Password from Login | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Click 'Forgot Password?'.<br>3. Check that uRL becomes `/forgot-password`, page title 'Forgot-Password \| Siddaganga'.<br>4. Check that heading 'Forgot Password', text 'Reset Your Password'.<br>5. Check that field 'Username / Email Address' (placeholder 'Enter your username or email address'), button 'Send OTP', link 'Go to Login'. |
| ADMIN-AUTH-015 | Authentication | Return to Login from Forgot Password | 1. Open /forgot-password.<br>2. Click 'Go to Login'.<br>3. Check that the login page (/login) is shown. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-016 | Authentication | Request a password-reset OTP for a registered account | 1. Open /forgot-password.<br>2. Enter the QA account username/email.<br>3. Click 'Send OTP'.<br>4. Check that a success message is shown and an OTP entry (and new password) step appears. (to be confirmed)<br>5. Check that an OTP email is delivered to the account's address. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-017 | Authentication | Forgot Password with empty / unregistered / malformed input | 1. Open /forgot-password.<br>2. Click 'Send OTP' with the field empty.<br>3. Enter 'abc@' and click 'Send OTP'.<br>4. Enter an unregistered email and click 'Send OTP'.<br>5. Check that empty and malformed inputs are rejected with a validation message. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that unregistered input shows a generic message without disclosing account existence. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-018 | Authentication | Reset password with invalid/expired OTP and weak password | 1. Complete 'Send OTP' for a test account.<br>2. Enter a wrong OTP and submit.<br>3. Request again, wait beyond OTP validity and submit the old OTP.<br>4. Enter the valid OTP with a weak password (e.g. '123').<br>5. Check that wrong/expired OTPs are rejected. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that weak passwords are rejected per policy. (to be confirmed)<br>7. Check that a valid OTP + compliant password resets the password and allows login with it. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-019 | Authentication | Switch to 'Log in with OTP' and back | 1. Open /login.<br>2. Click 'Log in with OTP'.<br>3. Click 'Log in With Email & Password'.<br>4. Check that oTP mode shows only 'Username / Email Address', button 'Send OTP', hint 'We'll Send a OTP to Your Email Address', link 'Log in With Email & Password' (URL stays /login). (to be confirmed)<br>5. Check that clicking the link returns to the password form. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-020 | Authentication | Log in with a valid email OTP | 1. Open /login and click 'Log in with OTP'.<br>2. Enter the QA account email.<br>3. Click 'Send OTP'.<br>4. Enter the OTP received by email and submit.<br>5. Check that an OTP input step appears after 'Send OTP'. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that with a correct OTP the user lands on /dashboard. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-AUTH-021 | Authentication | OTP login with wrong, expired or reused OTP | 1. Request an OTP.<br>2. Enter an incorrect OTP.<br>3. Enter the correct OTP after its expiry.<br>4. Reuse an OTP that was already used.<br>5. Use 'Resend OTP' if offered.<br>6. Check that incorrect, expired and reused OTPs are rejected with a clear message. (to be confirmed)<br>7. Check that resend issues a new OTP and invalidates the old one. (to be confirmed) |

## Role & Permission

| Scenario ID | Module | Use Case Title | Scenario Steps |
|---|---|---|---|
| ADMIN-ROLE-001 | Role & Permission | Open the User Management (All Users) screen | 1. Log in to Admin Web as Superadmin.<br>2. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>3. Wait for the page to finish loading.<br>4. Check that the browser URL is `/dashboard/user-management`.<br>5. Check that the header title reads 'Roles & Permission'. |
| ADMIN-ROLE-002 | Role & Permission | Reload User Management (All Users) via its direct URL | 1. While logged in, enter `https://dari-dev.siddagangatravels.com/dashboard/user-management` in the address bar and press Enter.<br>2. Press F5 to refresh the page.<br>3. Check that user Management (All Users) opens directly without redirecting to /login.<br>4. Check that after refresh the same screen and default view are displayed. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-003 | Role & Permission | Block User Management (All Users) URL without an authenticated session | 1. Open a new private/incognito browser window (no Admin Web session).<br>2. Navigate to `https://dari-dev.siddagangatravels.com/dashboard/user-management`.<br>3. Check that the user is redirected to `/login` (observed for the Contracted Clients route at session start). (to be confirmed)<br>4. Check that no data from the screen is displayed before login. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-004 | Role & Permission | Search User Management (All Users) using 'Search User or Role' with an existing value | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Copy a value from the first row (e.g. the name/ID column).<br>3. Type it into the 'Search User or Role' box (and press Enter or click the search icon if results do not update automatically).<br>4. Check that only rows containing the searched value are listed. (to be confirmed)<br>5. Check that the 'Showing x to y of N' total decreases accordingly. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-005 | Role & Permission | Search User Management (All Users) with special characters and leading/trailing spaces | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Enter '  %_<>'"  ' in 'Search User or Role'.<br>3. Clear the box, then enter the first row's name surrounded by spaces.<br>4. Check that no error page or console crash; special characters return 'No data available'. (to be confirmed)<br>5. Check that leading/trailing spaces are trimmed and the matching row is returned. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-006 | Role & Permission | Clear search on User Management (All Users) restores the full list | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Search for any value in 'Search User or Role'.<br>3. Delete the search text completely.<br>4. Check that the full list is shown again with the original 'Showing … of N' total. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-007 | Role & Permission | Filter User Management (All Users) by 'Status' dropdown | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Open the 'Status' dropdown.<br>3. Select each option in turn and observe the list.<br>4. Check – The dropdown lists: 'Select Status', 'All', 'Active', 'Inactive'.<br>5. Check that after selecting an option only rows with that value are listed and the total updates. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that selecting the 'All' option (if present) shows all rows again. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-008 | Role & Permission | '+ Add User' control on User Management (All Users) | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Click '+ Add User'.<br>3. Check that the related form/details panel opens. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-009 | Role & Permission | Open 'Access User' form on User Management (All Users) | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Click 'Access'.<br>3. Check that a dialog titled 'Access User' opens.<br>4. Check – Buttons: 'Access', 'Cancel'. |
| ADMIN-ROLE-010 | Role & Permission | Close 'Access User' without saving (Cancel / ✕ / Esc) | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Click 'Access'.<br>3. Enter any value in the first editable field.<br>4. Click 'Cancel' (repeat using the ✕ icon and the Esc key).<br>5. Check that the dialog closes and no record is created or changed (list total unchanged).<br>6. Check that re-opening the dialog shows empty/default values, not the abandoned input. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-011 | Role & Permission | Open 'Add More Permissions' form on User Management (All Users) | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Click 'Add More Permissions'.<br>3. Check that a dialog titled 'Add More Permissions' opens. |
| ADMIN-ROLE-012 | Role & Permission | Close 'Add More Permissions' without saving (Cancel / ✕ / Esc) | 1. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>2. Click 'Add More Permissions'.<br>3. Enter any value in the first editable field.<br>4. Click 'Cancel' (repeat using the ✕ icon and the Esc key).<br>5. Check that the dialog closes and no record is created or changed (list total unchanged).<br>6. Check that re-opening the dialog shows empty/default values, not the abandoned input. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-ROLE-013 | Role & Permission | Open the Role Management (All Roles) screen | 1. Log in to Admin Web as Superadmin.<br>2. In the left sidebar, click 'Role & Permission'.<br>3. On the page, click 'Role Management'.<br>4. Wait for the page to finish loading.<br>5. Check that the browser URL is `/dashboard/user-management`.<br>6. Open the rows-per-page dropdown in the table footer (default shown: '05').<br>4. Select each available value in turn.<br>5. Check that the table shows at most the selected number of rows and 'Showing 1 to n of N' updates. (to be confirmed) |

| ADMIN-FLEET-001 | Fleet & Vehicle Management | Open the Vehicle Management Portal screen | 1. Log in to Admin Web as Superadmin.<br>2. In the left sidebar, expand 'Fleet & Vehicle Management' and click 'Fleet & Vehicles'.<br>3. Wait for the page to finish loading.<br>4. Check that the browser URL is `/dashboard/fleet-vehicle-management`.<br>5. Check that the header title reads 'Vehicle Management Portal'.<br>6. Check – The summary cards shown are: Total Buses / Active, Approved & Track Live Trips, Vehicle Allocation Portal, Tarrif Management. |
| ADMIN-FLEET-002 | Fleet & Vehicle Management | Reload Vehicle Management Portal via its direct URL | 1. While logged in, enter `https://dari-dev.siddagangatravels.com/dashboard/fleet-vehicle-management` in the address bar and press Enter.<br>2. Press F5 to refresh the page.<br>3. Click the ⋮ icon in the ACTIONS column of the first lead row.<br>3. Click 'View Lead'.<br>4. Check that dialog 'Lead Details' with section 'Lead Information' (Lead ID, Client Name, Expected Value, Stage, Status, Assigned To) and section 'Inquiry Information' (Inquiry PNR).<br>5. Check – Buttons: close icon and 'Send To Inquiry'. Esc closes the dialog (observed). |
| ADMIN-LEAD-026 | Lead Management | Lead Details match the list row | 1. Click 'Lead Management' under FLEET & TRIPS in the left sidebar.<br>2. Note LEAD ID, CLIENT NAME, STAGE, EXPECTED VALUE, ASSIGNED TO and STATUS of the first row.<br>3. Click the ⋮ icon in the ACTIONS column of the first lead row.<br>4. Click 'View Lead'.<br>5. Check that all values in Lead Information equal the row values; Inquiry PNR is empty for leads not yet sent to inquiry. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-LEAD-027 | Lead Management | Send a lead to Inquiry | 1. Create a QA-TEST lead.<br>2. Click 'Lead Management' under FLEET & TRIPS in the left sidebar.<br>3. Open ⋮ on the QA-TEST lead and click 'Send To Inquiry' (or use the button in Lead Details).<br>4. Confirm if prompted. |
| ADMIN-LEAD-028 | Lead Management | Send the same lead to Inquiry twice | 1. Use a QA-TEST lead already sent to inquiry.<br>2. Click 'Lead Management' under FLEET & TRIPS in the left sidebar.<br>3. Click 'Send To Inquiry' again.<br>4. Check that a second inquiry is not created; a message explains the lead was already sent. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-LEAD-029 | Lead Management | Assign a lead to a user | 1. Create a QA-TEST lead.<br>2. Click 'Lead Management' under FLEET & TRIPS in the left sidebar.<br>3. Open ⋮ on it and click 'Assign'.<br>4. Select an assignee and confirm.<br>5. Check that aSSIGNED TO shows the selected user in the list and in Lead Details. The Assign dialog was not opened during exploration. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-LEAD-030 | Lead Management | Cancel deleting a lead | 1. Create a QA-TEST lead.<br>2. Click 'Lead Management' under FLEET & TRIPS in the left sidebar.<br>3. Open ⋮ on it and click 'Delete'.<br>4. Cancel the confirmation. |
| ADMIN-LEAD-031 | Lead Management | Delete a lead | 1. Create a QA-TEST lead.<br>2. Click 'Lead Management' under FLEET & TRIPS in the left sidebar.<br>3. Note the total.<br>4. Open ⋮ on it, click 'Delete' and confirm.<br>5. Check that success message; the lead is removed and the total decreases by 1. Behaviour for leads already sent to inquiry. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-LEAD-032 | Lead Management | Update a lead's Stage and Status after creation | 1. Click 'Lead Management' under FLEET & TRIPS in the left sidebar.<br>2. Click the ⋮ icon in the ACTIONS column of the first lead row.<br>3. Look for a way to change Stage (Prospecting → Qualification → Proposal → Negotiation → Closed Won/Lost) or Status. |

NO., CLIENT NAME, EMAIL ADDRESS, PHONE NUMBER, CLIENT CATEGORY, TOTAL BOOKINGS, UPCOMING BOOKINGS, LAST INVOICE, OUTSTANDING, LOYALTY, RCM, STATUS, ACTIONS.<br>7. Check that only rows containing the searched value are listed. (to be confirmed)<br>5. Check that the 'Showing x to y of N' total decreases accordingly. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-CLIENT-189 | Client Management | Search Contracted – Client Requests with special characters and leading/trailing spaces | 1. In the left sidebar, expand 'Client Management' and click 'Client Management', 'Contracted Client', then the 'Client Requests' card.<br>2. Enter '  %_<>'"  ' in 'Search Client Info'.<br>3. Clear the box, then enter the first row's name surrounded by spaces.<br>4. Check that no error page or console crash; special characters return 'No data available'. (to be confirmed)<br>5. Check that leading/trailing spaces are trimmed and the matching row is returned. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-CLIENT-190 | Client Management | Clear search on Contracted – Client Requests restores the full list | 1. In the left sidebar, expand 'Client Management' and click 'Client Management', 'Contracted Client', then the 'Client Requests' card.<br>2. Search for any value in 'Search Client Info'.<br>3. Delete the search text completely.<br>4. Check that the full list is shown again with the original 'Showing … of N' total. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-CLIENT-191 | Client Management | Filter Contracted – Client Requests by 'Status' dropdown | 1. In the left sidebar, expand 'Client Management' and click 'Client Management', 'Contracted Client', then the 'Client Requests' card.<br>2. Open the 'Status' dropdown.<br>3. Select each option in turn and observe the list.<br>4. Check – The dropdown lists: 'Select Status', 'All', 'Active', 'Inactive'.<br>5. Check that after selecting an option only rows with that value are listed and the total updates. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that selecting the 'All' option (if present) shows all rows again. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-CLIENT-192 | Client Management | Change rows per page on Contracted – Client Requests | 1. In the left sidebar, expand 'Client Management' and click 'Client Management', 'Contracted Client', then the 'Client Requests' card.<br>2. Open the rows-per-page dropdown in the table footer (default shown: '05').<br>3. Select each available value in turn.<br>4. Check that the table shows at most the selected number of rows and 'Showing 1 to n of N' updates. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-CLIENT-193 | Client Management | Navigate pages of the Contracted – Client Requests list | 1. In the left sidebar, expand 'Client Management' and click 'Client Management', 'Contracted Client', then the 'Client Requests' card.<br>2. Click 'Go to next page'.<br>3. Click 'Go to last page'.<br>4. Click 'Go to previous page'.<br>5. Click 'Go to first page'.<br>6. Check that each click loads the corresponding page and 'Showing x to y of N' updates. (to be confirmed)<br>7. Check that on page 1, 'Go to first page' and 'Go to previous page' are disabled. (to be confirmed)<br>8. Check that on the last page, 'Go to next page' and 'Go to last page' are disabled and the last row number equals N. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-CLIENT-194 | Client Management | Change record status via status button (Active/Inactive) in the row on Contracted – Client Requests | 1. In the left sidebar, expand 'Client Management' and click 'Client Management', 'Contracted Client', then the 'Client Requests' card.<br>2. On a test record, click the status button (Active/Inactive) in the row.<br>3. Confirm if a prompt appears.<br>4. Filter the list by the new status.<br>5. Check that the status label changes (e.g. Active → Inactive) and a success message appears. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that the record appears under the matching status filter. (to be confirmed)<br>7. Check that inactive records are not offered in dependent dropdowns elsewhere. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-CLIENT-195 | Client Management | Export the Contracted – Client Requests list using 'Export PDF' | 1. In the left sidebar, expand 'Client Management' and click 'Client Management', 'Contracted Client', then the 'Client Requests' card.<br>2. Optionally apply a filter/search.<br>3. Click 'Export PDF'.<br>4. Check that a PDF file downloads. (to be confirmed)<br>5. Check that the PDF contains the same columns and rows as the current (filtered) list. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that export with an empty list produces an empty report or a message. (to be confirmed) |
| ADMIN-CLIENT-196 | Client Management | Filter Request Category by 'Status' dropdown | 1. In the left sidebar, expand 'Client Management' and click 'Request Category'.<br>2. Open the 'Status' dropdown.<br>3. Select each option in turn and observe the list.<br>4. Check – The dropdown lists: 'Select Status', 'All', 'Active', 'Inactive'.<br>5. Check that after selecting an option only rows with that value are listed and the total updates. (to be confirmed)<br>6. Check that selecting the 'All' option (if present) shows all rows again. (to be confirmed) |

| ADMIN-CAT-001 | Category Management | Open the Main Category screen | 1. Log in to Admin Web as Superadmin.<br>2. In the left sidebar, expand 'Category Management' and click 'Main Category'.<br>3. Wait for the page to finish loading.<br>4. Check that the browser URL is `/dashboard/category-management/main-category`.<br>5. Check that the header title reads 'Main Category'.<br>6. Check – The list table shows the columns: S.NO, CATEGORY ID, CATEGORY NAME, DESCRIPTION, DEFAULT UOM, STATUS, ACTION. |

While logged in, enter `https://dari-dev.siddagangatravels.com/dashboard/category-management/main-category` in the address bar and press Enter.<br>2. Press F5 to refresh the page.<br>3. Check that main Category opens directly without redirecting to /login.<br>4. Check that after refresh the same screen and default view are displayed. (to be confirmed) |
